'7' : '2')\n    localStorage.setItem('roleCode', role)\n    localStorage.setItem('username', '课时核验')\n  }, teacher ? 'teacher' : 'student')\n  await page.route('**/api/**', async route => {\n    const request = route.request(), path = new URL(request.url()).pathname\n    calls.push({ path, method: request.method(), body: request.postData() ? request.postDataJSON() : null })\n    const ok = data => route.fulfill({ json: { code: '200', data } })\n    if (path === '/api/courses/1') return ok({ id: 1, title: '算法课程' })\n    if (path === '/api/courses') return ok({ records: [{ id: 1, title: '算法课程', teacherId: 7 }] })\n    if (path === '/api/courses/1/chapters') return ok([{ id: 1, title: '基础' }])\n    if (path === '/api/chapters/1/lessons') return ok([{ id: 11, title: '搜索算法', resourceType: 'text', content: '二分查找每次将搜索区间缩小一半。' }, { id: 12, title: '排序算法', resourceType: 'text', content: '排序将元素按顺序排列。' }])\n    if (path === '/api/courses/1/experience') return ok({ textbook: [] })\n    if (path === '/api/rag/courses/1/documents') return ok(empty ? [] : [{ id: 'd1', documentId: 'd1', documentVersion: 1, title: '算法讲义' }])\n    if (path === '/api/rag/courses/1/documents/d1/versions/1') return ok({ documentId: 'd1', documentVersion: 1, title: '算法讲义', chunks: [{ chunkId: 'c1', content: '二分查找每轮减半。', page: 3 }] })\n    if (path === '/api/rag/knowledge-bases') return ok(teacher ? [{ id: 'kb1', owner_id: 7, name: '教师资料', knowledge_count: 1 }] : [])\n    if (path === '/api/rag/knowledge-bases/kb1/documents') return ok([{ id: 'd1', title: '算法讲义', file_name: '算法.pdf', document_version: 1, parse_status: 'success', chunk_count: 1 }])\n    if (path === '/api/rag/sessions') return ok([])\n    if (path === '/api/rag/chat/stream') {\n      if (delayed) await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 800))\n      const result = { sessionId: 's1', answer: empty ? '选中文本说明了逐次减半。' : '二分查找每轮减半。[1]', mode: empty ? 'selection' : 'course', status: empty ? 'SELECTION_ONLY' : 'ANSWERED', actualMode: empty ? 'NONE' : 'HYBRID', citations: empty ? [] : [{ chunkId: 'c1', documentId: 'd1', documentVersion: 1, title: '算法讲义', content: '二分查找每轮减半。', page: 3, citation: '[1]' }] }\n      return route.fulfill({ contentType: 'text/event-stream', body: `event: complete\\\\ndata: ${JSON.stringify(result)}\\\\n\\\\nevent: done\\\\ndata: {}\\\\n\\\\n` }).catch(() => {})\n    }\n    if (path.includes('/notes')) return ok({ title: '笔记', contentMd: '已有笔记' })\n    return ok([])\n  })\n  await page.goto(teacher ? '/teacher-knowledge-base' : '/course-learn/1?classId=1&lessonId=11')\n  return calls\n}\n\nasync function selectLessonText(page) {\n  await page.locator('.lesson-main-card .page-block').first().evaluate(el => {\n    const paragraph = [...el.querySelectorAll('p')].find(p => p.textContent.includes('二分查找'))\n    const range = document.createRange()\n    range.selectNodeContents(paragraph)\n    const selection = window.getSelection()\n    selection.removeAllRanges()\n    selection.addRange(range)\n    paragraph.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('mouseup', { bubbles: true }))\n  })\n  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '解释这段', exact: true }).click()\n}\n\ntest('selected text survives focus and is sent with current course RAG scope', async ({ page }) => {\n  const calls = await fixture(page)\n  await selectLessonText(page)\n  await expect(page.locator('.selection-card')).toContainText('二分查找')\n  await page.getByPlaceholder('输入问题，或直接解释选中文本').fill('为什么每次减半？')\n  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '发送', exact: true }).click()\n  await expect(page.locator('.quick-chat-card .chat-message.assistant')).toContainText('二分查找每轮减半。')\n  const sent = calls.find(call => call.path === '/api/rag/chat/stream').body\n  expect(sent.courseId).toBe(1)\n  expect(sent.lessonId).toBe(11)\n  expect(sent.selectedText).toContain('二分查找')\n  expect(sent.query).toBe('为什么每次减半？')\n  expect(sent.knowledgeBaseIds || []).toEqual([])\n  expect(calls.filter(call => call.path === '/api/rag/sessions' && call.method === 'POST')).toHaveLength(0)\n  await page.locator('.quick-chat-card').getByRole('button', { name: /算法讲义/ }).click()\n  await expect(page.locator('.source-text')).toContainText('二分查找每轮减半。')\n  expect(calls.some(call => call.path === '/api/rag/chunks/c1')).toBe(false)\n})\n\ntest('empty course still explains selected text with explicit selection-only mode', async ({ page }) => {\n  await fixture(page, { empty: true })\n  await selectLessonText(page)\n  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '发送', exact: true }).click()\n  await expect(page.locator('.quick-chat-card .chat-message.assistant')).toContainText('仅依据选中文本')\n  await expect(page.locator('.quick-chat-card .rag-citations')).toHaveCount(0)\n})\n\ntest('switching lessons cancels old answer and clears selected text', async ({ page }) => {\n  await fixture(page, { delayed: true })\n  await selectLessonText(page)\n  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '发送', exact: true }).click()\n  await page.getByText('排序算法', { exact: true }).click()\n  await page.getByRole('tab', { name: '问答', exact: true }).click()\n  await page.waitForTimeout(1000)\n  await expect(page.locator('.quick-chat-card .chat-message')).toHaveCount(0)\n  await expect(page.locator('.selection-card')).toHaveCount(0)\n})\n\ntest('teacher authorizes document to a course with original download disabled', async ({ page }) => {\n  const calls = await fixture(page, { teacher: true })\n  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '课程授权', exact: true }).click()\n  await page.getByPlaceholder('选择课程').click()\n  await page.getByRole('option', { name: '算法课程', exact: true }).click()\n  await page.getByRole('button', { name: '授权阅读', exact: true }).click()\n  await expect.poll(() => calls.some(call => call.path === '/api/rag/documents/d1/grants')).toBe(true)\n  expect(calls.find(call => call.path === '/api/rag/documents/d1/grants').body).toEqual({ courseId: 1, documentVersion: 1, readExcerpt: true, downloadAllowed: false })\n})\n''',encoding='utf-8')\np=Path('.codex_tmp/lesson-rag-frontend');p.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)\n(p/'playwright.config.mjs').write_text(\"import config from } from '@/composables/useNativeRag'\nimport { getCourseDocuments, ragList } from '@/api/rag'\nimport RagConversation from '@/components/knowledge/RagConversation.vue'\n\nconst props = defineProps({\n  courseId: { type: Number, required: true },\n  lessonId: { type: Number, required: true },\n  courseTitle: String,\n  lessonTitle: String,\n  selectedText: { type: String, default: '' }\n})\nconst emit = defineEmits(['update:selectedText'])\nconst userStore = useUserStore()\nconst {\n  messages, sessions, loading, currentSessionId, progress, error, cancel,\n  resetCourseContext, loadSessions, restoreSession, removeSession, sendMessage\n} = useNativeRag()\nconst question = ref('')\nconst historyId = ref('')\nconst documents = ref([])\nconst documentError = ref('')\nconst documentsLoading = ref(false)\nconst selection = ref(props.selectedText)\nconst canUseAssistant = computed(() => userStore.isLoggedIn && props.courseId > 0 && props.lessonId > 0)\nconst canSend = computed(() => canUseAssistant.value && Boolean(question.value.trim() || selection.value) && !loading.value)\nconst currentSessions = computed(() => sessions.value.filter(session =>\n  Number(session.courseId) === props.courseId && Number(session.lessonId) === props.lessonId\n))\nlet scopeVersion = 0\n\nwatch(() => props.selectedText, value => { selection.value = value })\nwatch(() => [props.courseId, '')\n}\n\nfunction newConversation() {\n  resetCourseContext(props.courseId, props.lessonId)\n  historyId.value = ''\n  question.value = ''\n  clearSelection()\n}\n\nasync function loadScope() {\n  const version = ++scopeVersion\n  newConversation()\n  documents.value = []\n  documentError.value = ''\n  documentsLoading.value = true\n  try {\n    const list = ragList(await getCourseDocuments(props.courseId))\n    if (version === scopeVersion) documents.value = list\n  } catch (failure) {\n    if (version === scopeVersion) documentError.value = failure?.message || '课程资料暂不可用，请重试'\n  } finally {\n    if (version === scopeVersion) documentsLoading.value = false\n  }\n  await loadSessions()\n}\n\nasync function switchHistory(id) {\n  if (!id) {\n    newConversation()\n    return\n  }\n  const session = currentSessions.value.find(item => item.id === id)\n  if (!session) return\n  clearSelection()\n  try {\n    await restoreSession(session)\n  } catch {\n    newConversation()\n  }\n}\n\nasync function deleteConversation() {\n  await removeSession(historyId.value)\n  newConversation()\n}\n\nasync function handleSend() {\n  if (!canSend.value) query, query, selectedText)\n    if (version !== scopeVersion) return\n    historyId.value = currentSessionId.value || ''\n    await loadSessions()\n  } catch {\n    if (version === scopeVersion) hashlib,json,datetime\np=Path('docs/competition/development/agent-records/2026-11-17-10039-implementation/frontend')\nreport='''# 课时 RAG 问答前端实施记录\n\n实施日期为 2026-11-17，工作区为 D:/ai-learning-collaboration-verified。\n任务覆盖课时选文与 RAG、课程资料授权入口和共享问答展示，沿用已有页面与课程接口。开始时已存在的逐页核验及其 API 改动继续保留。\n\n## 功能\n\n- 学习页正文与笔记编辑器选文后提供“解释这段”，固定所选内容并切到课时问答，可移除选文。\n- 问答携带当前 courseId、lessonId、独立 query 和 selectedText。首次直接请求流式问答，课程模式不创建私有库会话。\n- 展示当前课程授权资料数量，支持课时历史、新对话、删除、停止生成和模型故障重试。切课时清除选文并阻止旧回调恢复内容。\n- 共享 RagConversation 展示知识库及课时消息、公式、流式状态和来源。课程引用用课程授权的文档版本接口读取出处，保持原件下载入口受限。\n- 教师资料列表增加课程授权与撤销入口，读取现有 /courses 列表，后端按教师身份过滤可维护课程。授权默认 readExcerpt=true、downloadAllowed=false。\n\n## 实际验证\n\n| 检查 | 结果 |\n| --- | --- |\n| 新课时功能受控浏览器测试 | 最终 8 项通过，见 final-lesson-ui.log |\n| 知识库、K02、K03 受控浏览器回归 | 25 项通过，与当时 7 项新测试合跑共 32 项，见 green-ui.log |\n| 图文引用与学习布局回归 | 18 项通过，见 visual-layout-ui.log |\n| 公式渲染 | 8 项通过，见 formula-rendering.log |\n| 最终生产构建 | 通过，见 build.log。存在既有第三方注解及大 chunk 警告 |\n| diff 空白检查 | 通过 |\n\n按不重复用例计，本轮浏览器覆盖为 51 项。受控响应测试验证界面和接口接线，真实模型联测由根任务执行和归档。\n\n## 失败与修正\n\n首轮测试夹具仅拦截 /api 路径，课程原有接口使用 /courses，导致未加载正文。修正拦截范围后，确认旧页面缺少“解释这段”入口，测试在该步骤失败，再实现选文功能。\n选文按钮初放右下角被已有浮动助手遮挡，实际点击测试失败后将其上移。\n教师授权测试先按 placeholder、后按 combobox 点击，都受到 Element Plus 内部占位层影响，改为键盘 Enter 打开下拉，验证可访问交互。\n前端初稿给普通私有库请求也带 selectedText 空串，根任务审查发现兼容问题后改为只在课程模式发送该字段，相关知识库回归通过。\n\n失败输出保留在真实脱敏会话记录中。早期自动截图目录被后续运行复用，未宣称保存了全部失败图片。\n\n## 会话与运行边界\n\n子会话 ID 为 01a1e846-d63c-7811-aa03-9d5fef8b0b29。核对原始 JSONL，113 行结束继承的父会话，114 行开始子 Agent 身份与实际执行边界，导出显式指定 --start-line 114。首个实际工具调用在源行 123。\n子会话未单列保存收到的 NEW_TASK 消息，委派原文由父会话工具调用保存。本目录另存收到的委派文本以供核查。导出过滤隐藏推理和秘密，仅代表指定快照。\n本轮测试服务使用 5174。另启动 24739 前端，临时配置位于 .codex_tmp/lesson-rag-frontend/live-vite.config.mjs，通过 VITE_API_BASE_URL 指向隔离后端 18039，供根任务真实联测。根任务决定最终演示服务的保留和停止。\n'''\n(p/'REPORT.md').write_text(report,encoding='utf-8')\n(p/'delegation.txt').write_text('负责课时RAG前端实施(含测试先RED)。独占LessonQuickChat.vue、CourseLearn.vue、useNativeRag.js、新增RagConversation.vue共享组件、RagCitations.vue/RagAnswerStatus.vue、NativeKnowledgeWorkspace.vue(注意已有未提交改动必须保留)、相关e2e新测试和src/api/rag.js(亦有既有改动保留)。需求见计划，课时内选文解释+RAG、当前课程授权资料、选文卡片移除、无命中有选文模式、历史/停止/新会话/切课取消防旧响应。复用知识库chat消息展示抽成组件双方使用。教师workspace最小文档授权/撤销界面，选教师可维护课程，默认downloadAllowed false复用POST /api/rag/documents/{id}/grants 和DELETE。后端我做。约定扩展chat payload courseId,lessonId,selectedText(<=1500),query(<=2000),knowledgeBaseIds[]旧请求兼容；课程初次发送sessionId null自动建，不调用owner createSession。GET ## 源行 498 · 2026-11-17T07:13:26.379Z · tool_call / exec

call_id: `call_e099b18fbbef45568a9ebe8b603cca4e`；调用源行：498

    text(await tools.exec_command({cmd:"@'\nconst { chromium } = require('../../ai-learning-frontend/node_modules/playwright')\n;(async () => {\n  const browser = await chromium.launch({ channel: 'chrome', headless: true })\n  try {\n    const page = await browser.newPage({ viewport: { width: 1440, height: 1000 } })\n    await page.goto('http://127.0.0.1:24739/login?redirect=%2Fteacher-knowledge-base')\n    await page.getByPlaceholder('请输入用户名').fill('teacher')\n    await page.getByPlaceholder('请输入密码').fill('123456')\n    await page.getByRole('button', { name: '登录', exact: true }).click()\n    await page.getByRole('button', { name: '课程授权', exact: true }).first().click()\n    await page.getByRole('combobox', { name: '选择课程' }).press('Enter')\n    await page.getByRole('option', { name: '课时RAG联测课程', exact: true }).click()\n    await page.getByRole('button', { name: '撤销授权', exact: true }).waitFor()\n    await page.waitForTimeout(500)\n    await page.screenshot({ path: 'docs/competition/development/agent-records/2026-11-17-10039-implementation/frontend/live-teacher-authorization.png', fullPage: true })\n    console.log('Existing course grant displayed; screenshot refreshed')\n  } finally { await browser.close() }\n})()\n'@ | Set-Content .codex_tmp/lesson-rag-frontend/teacher-screenshot.cjs\nnode .codex_tmp/lesson-rag-frontend/teacher-screenshot.cjs","yield_time_ms":1000,"max_output_tokens":300}));
